<div class="product-single__description rte">
<p> </p>
Hydrate in style with your personalized Official Love Island Personalized Water Bottle! Your name will be applied to the front of the bottle. The typeface used in the show is a script font. Some names containing double letters such as "mm" will be fluid and curvaceous. Sizing and positioning of text may vary. Character limit is 8 characters.
<p> Product Details:</p>
<ul>
<li>740ml</li>
<li>Clear outer</li>
<li>Twist-on top</li>
<li>Finger-loop</li>
<li>100% BPA Free Plastic</li>
<li>Removable fruit infuser to add flavor to your drink</li>
<li>Drinking pipe inside bottle</li>
<li>H: 19cm/7" W: 7cm/3" D: 7cm/3" </li>
<li>Features the Official Love Island stamp on the base</li>
<li>Not dishwasher safe</li>
</ul>
<p>Care Instructions: To look after your bottle we recommend wiping clean with warm water and soap. Do not use bleach to clean the bottle. The bottles are not dishwasher safe. Always securely screw on the base before filling the bottle. Please do not fill the bottle with hot/carbonated beverages.</p>
</div>

<script>
window.onload = function() {
 jQuery(function($) {
   var offset = 300;
   var duration = 500;
   $(window).scroll(function() {
     if ($(this).scrollTop() > offset) {
       $('.back-to-top').fadeIn(duration);
     } 
     else {
       $('.back-to-top').fadeOut(duration);
     }
   });
   $('.back-to-top').unbind('click.smoothscroll').bind('click', function(e) {
     e.preventDefault();
     $('html, body').animate( { scrollTop: 0 }, duration);
     return false;
   })
 });
}
</script>
